F[11652572] = function(arg, arg2, arg3)

	return
end

	local n = #targets
	local flag = 1 < 0

	local quest = v.Quest

		v.Quest = quest
	end

	end

		local tt6 = table.pack(V.slots[upval_8].CompleteQuest(quest, v4.CFrame, v3))
		local tt7 = table.pack(u({ tt6[1] }))
		return
	end

	local tt5 = table.pack(u({ tt4[1] }))
	return
end
